# Reservoirs Act 1975

> **Warning: changes not yet applied to this text (15).** Text as at 2022-03-25. See https://www.legislation.gov.uk/changes/affected/ukpga/1975/23
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland.

*Extent: E+W+S*

Reservoirs Act 1975

1975 c.23

An Act to make futher provision against escapes of water from large reservoirs or from lakes or lochs artificially created or enlarged.

\[8th May 1975\][^c709075][^c709076] [^c709077][^c709078] [^c709079][^c709080]

## Operation of Act, and administration (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### A1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Large raised reservoir”: England and Wales (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7f364a99fa5659a5b1b7ac24f87741ed][^c-b8f7cceedc4f47bcf471bf78ee706966][^c-429f0119929031db3d4b10482cac84e6][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In this Act “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**large raised reservoir *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**” means— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] a large, raised structure designed or used for collecting and storing water, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] a large, raised lake or other area capable of storing water which was created or enlarged by artificial means. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A structure or area is “raised” if it is capable of holding water above the natural level of any part of the surrounding land. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-42773af72fb6845a7430c3d6b8448482][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A raised structure or area is “large” if it is capable of holding 10,000 cubic metres of water above the natural level of any part of the surrounding land. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister must make regulations about how to calculate capacity for the purpose of subsection (3) (and “natural level” and “surrounding land” are to be construed in accordance with the regulations). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister may by regulations provide for a structure or area to be treated as “large” by reason of proximity to, or actual or potential communication with, another structure or area. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-42773af72fb6845a7430c3d6b8448482][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In making regulations under subsection (5) the Minister shall aim to ensure that a structure or area is treated as large under the regulations only if 10,000 or more cubic metres of water might be released as a result of the proximity or communication mentioned in that subsection. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister may by order substitute a different volume of water for the volume specified in subsection (3) or (6). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister may by regulations provide for specified things not to be treated as large raised reservoirs for the purposes of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A reference to a large raised reservoir includes a reference to anything used or designed to contain the water or control its flow. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Ambit of Act and interpretation. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c709085][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In relation to any reservoir “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**undertakers *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**” means for purposes of this Act— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] in the case of a reservoir that is or, when constructed, is to be managed and operated *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] by the Environment Agency, the Natural Resources Body for Wales (the “[^c-5c8c3da3039a7b620a9553837b6f26eb] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]NRBW *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]”) or a water undertaker, the Environment Agency, the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or, as the case may be, the water *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] undertaker[^c709088] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] in any other case— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] if the reservoir is used or intended to be used for the purposes of any undertaking, the persons for the time being carrying on that undertaking; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] if the reservoir is not so used or intended to be used, the owners or lessees of the reservoir. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-af07aa855999548ff010c5e1f254d1c4][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The “area” of the Environment Agency, in its capacity as a relevant authority for purposes of this Act, is the whole of England *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-905ed21b43bef933263344274e3c97c9].... *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4C (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-9604e9e6fba0e2f7779ce9602cd4ffe7][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The “area” of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], in its capacity as a relevant authority for the purposes of this Act, is the whole of Wales. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] This Act shall not be taken as conferring on any person a claim to damages in respect of a breach by undertakers of their obligations under this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3ff7ade6c7e25fc91aa87e723005a4ec][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In this Act, “appropriate agency” means— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] in relation to reservoirs in England, the Environment Agency; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] in relation to reservoirs in Wales, the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The expressions listed in column 1 of Schedule 1 to this Act have for purposes of this Act the meanings given to them by the provisions of this Act shown in column 2 of the Schedule. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Registration of large raised reservoirs, and enforcement of Act, by local authorities. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0cd03167047f8d0ecbe441d2a8a30cee][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709095][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The relevant authorities for purposes of this Act shall be, in England[^c-0c5ec8a503af1919dab02f3fc699b27e] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-07bbb7634136462dee65b19f3e523c12]... the Environment Agency *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , in Wales the[^c-77f167a18d4ae433f52221ccca27da48] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] and, in Scotland, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] councils constituted under section 2 of the Local Government[^c709099] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] etc. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] (Scotland) Act 1994 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] It shall be the duty of each *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority[^c-fc6601e0d1eee8ce64cd7750458dae35] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to establish and maintain for their area a register showing the large raised reservoirs situated wholly or partly in the area, and giving the prescribed information about each of them; and— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the register maintained by a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] under this subsection and copies of it or a prescribed part of it shall be kept at such place or places as may be prescribed; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the register so maintained, and any copy required under sub-paragraph (a) above to be kept at any place, shall be available for inspection at all reasonable times by any person. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2A (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If it appears to the Secretary of State that the inclusion of any information in the register maintained under subsection (2) above by the Environment Agency *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or the[^c-ee080b3c3e8be1ee85054e3e3a880f98] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] would be contrary to the interests of national security, he may direct the Agency *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or the[^c-a983a20bf8041457196daad73b4628d1] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] not to include that information in the register. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2C (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister may make regulations about registration under this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2D (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Regulations under subsection (2C) may, in particular, include provision about— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the information to be registered, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the time by which information, or changes to information, must be registered. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2E (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The regulations may require an undertaker to notify the relevant authority about— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] proposed alterations to which section 6 applies; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] a proposal to abandon a large raised reservoir under section 14; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the appointment, or termination of appointment, of an engineer for a purpose of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] It shall be for the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] in whose area a reservoir is situated, if they are not themselves the undertakers, to secure that the undertakers observe and comply with the requirements of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where a reservoir extends into the areas of more than one *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], then unless one of those authorities are the undertakers, the authority charged under subsection (3) above with securing that the undertakers observe and comply with the requirements of this Act shall be such one of those authorities as may be agreed between them or, in default of agreement, may be determined by the Secretary of State. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where a reservoir extends into the areas of more than one *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], and one of those authorities are the undertakers, subsections (3) and (4) above shall apply as if the reservoir did not extend into the area of that one of the authorities. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] For purposes of this Act “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**enforcement authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**” means, in relation to a reservoir, the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] charged under subsection (3) above with securing that the undertakers observe and comply with the requirements of this Act (and, where the context so requires, includes the authority that would be so charged if the reservoir were a large raised reservoir); and accordingly the provisions of this Act relating to the enforcement authority for a reservoir do not apply in the case of a reservoir if a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] are the undertakers and the reservoir is situated wholly in the area of that authority. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] There shall be paid out of money provided by Parliament any increase attributable to this Act in the amounts so payable under any enactment by way of rate support grant. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 2A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Consideration of risk and provisional designation: England and Wales (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7f5ef75ad49d7fc14cd2b26bd3d97733][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Each *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority[^c-500b5dbad7e2ffd27c6b5ec426f8098c] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] shall, at such intervals as may be prescribed, make to the Secretary of State a report giving such information as may be prescribed as to the steps taken by them as enforcement authority to secure that undertakers observe and comply with the requirements of this Act or as to the steps taken by them to observe and comply with those requirements as undertakers for any reservoir situated wholly in their area. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If it appears to the Secretary of State that he should do so, the Secretary of State may cause an inquiry to be held into the question whether a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] have failed to perform any of their functions as such under this Act in a case where they ought to have performed them. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If, after an inquiry under subsection (2) above or without such an inquiry, the Secretary of State is satisfied that a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] have failed to perform any of their functions as such under this Act in a case where they ought to have performed them, the Secretary of State may make an order declaring the authority to be in default; and the order may, for the purpose of remedying the default, direct the authority to perform such of their functions as may be specified in the order, and may specify the manner in which, and the time or times within which, those functions are to be performed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An order under this section may be varied or revoked by a further order of the Secretary of State. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In Scotland, if the authority declared to be in default by an order under this section fail to comply with any requirement thereof within the time limited thereby for compliance with that requirement, the Court of Session may, on the application of the Lord Advocate on behalf of the Secretary of State, order specific performance of the functions in respect of which there has been default, and do otherwise as to the court appears to be just. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Qualification of engineers. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] There shall be a panel of civil engineers for the purposes of this Act, or such number of different panels for different purposes of this Act or for different classes of reservoir as, after consultation, the Secretary of State may from time to time determine; and any reference in this Act to a qualified civil engineer is a reference to a civil engineer who is a member of the panel constituted under this section or, if there are for the time being more than one such panel, a member of the appropriate panel. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any civil engineer may apply in the prescribed manner to be placed on any panel; and if the application is accompanied by the prescribed fee (if any), and the Secretary of State is satisfied after consultation that the applicant is qualified and fit to be placed on that panel, the Secretary of State shall appoint him to be a member of the panel. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any appointment under subsection (2) above shall be for a term of five years; but an engineer whose appointment expires may be re-appointed under that subsection for a further term. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any appointment to a panel shall also lapse on the abolition or alteration of that panel; but— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] before abolishing or altering any panel the Secretary of State shall give to the engineers for the time being on the panel such notice as he considers reasonable to allow them to apply for appointment to any other panel constituted or to be constituted wholly or partly for the same purposes; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] subject to subsection (5) below, a person who is appointed to act for any purpose of this Act (otherwise than as supervising engineer) at a time when he is a qualified civil engineer for that purpose may, on ceasing under this subsection to be a member of the appropriate panel, continue for not more than four years to act by virtue of that appointment and shall be for the purpose a qualified civil engineer. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Secretary of State may remove an engineer from any panel, or direct that he shall no longer be qualified to act by virtue of subsection (4)(b) above, if the Secretary of State is satisfied after consultation that the engineer is not fit to remain on the panel or not fit so to act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] References in this section to consultation by the Secretary of State are references to consultation with the President of the Institution of Civil Engineers or, if that institution appoint a committee for the relevant purpose, with that committee. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Secretary of State may reimburse to the Institution of Civil Engineers any expenses incurred by the Institution for purposes of this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any expenses of the Secretary of State under this section shall be defrayed out of money provided by Parliament; and any fees received by the Secretary of State under subsection (2) above shall be paid into the Consolidated Fund. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If at any time the Institution of Civil Engineers ceases to exist references in this section to that institution or its President shall have effect as references to the prescribed institution or head of the prescribed institution. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(10 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-1b517ffa6cd5d4e4ed4f24607cb4fbfd][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Secretary of State and the Welsh Ministers acting jointly may establish one or more panels of engineers under this section (“joint panels”). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(11 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] For the purposes of anything done in relation to a joint panel, a reference in this section to the Secretary of State is a reference to the Secretary of State and the Welsh Ministers acting jointly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## New, enlarged and restored reservoirs (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Construction or enlargement of reservoirs. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709102][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] No large raised reservoir shall be constructed (whether as a new reservoir or by the alteration of an *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] existing structure or area *(Extent: E)*[^c-662fe7fda43ff254fa7009703f5a9327] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that is not a large raised reservoir) or shall be altered so as to increase *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or decrease[^c-322115f0b0e3d60325314f6bbd0f61ae] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] its capacity, unless a qualified civil engineer (“ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**the construction engineer *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**”) is employed to design and supervise the construction or alteration; and where the use of a reservoir as a reservoir has been abandoned, and the reservoir is to be brought back into use after being altered so as to increase *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or decrease *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] its capacity, that shall be treated for purposes of this Act as the construction of a new reservoir. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where a large raised reservoir is constructed as a new reservoir, it shall not be used for the storage of water, or be filled wholly or partially with water, otherwise than in accordance with the certificate of the construction engineer responsible for its construction (or, in the event of any alteration to it, in accordance with subsection (4) below). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where a large raised reservoir is constructed by the alteration of an *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] existing structure or area *(Extent: E)*[^c-2bb20d458241b4e916fc2a6507ec6dcf] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that is not a large raised reservoir, the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-b55404ac7a0d3a97168a5fb69d788e71]... reservoir shall not be used for the storage of water, or be filled wholly or partially with water, otherwise than in accordance with the certificate of the construction engineer responsible for the construction of the reservoir (or, in the event of any alteration to it, in accordance with subsection (4) below). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where a large raised reservoir is altered so as to increase *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or decrease[^c-b2c4d63fca04083aaad11083c3a782ec] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] its capacity, then from the time when the construction engineer responsible for the alteration gives any certificate for the reservoir, the reservoir shall not be used for the storage of water, or be filled wholly or partially with water, otherwise than in accordance with the certificate of that construction engineer (or, in the event of any further alteration to it, in accordance with this subsection as it applies on that alteration). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the construction or alteration of a reservoir is by this section required to be supervised by a construction engineer, the reservoir shall be under the supervision of the construction engineer until he gives his final certificate for the reservoir. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-5480eee91925299cbfc4320c5f10fcd0]... *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dab76db5de0ee933bbca15165652d1ed][^c-5de2d17a2652b70b65b40a6d1cb91dc4][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] This section and sections 7 and 8 do not apply in relation to alterations, or proposed alterations, to decrease the capacity of a large raised reservoir so that it is incapable of holding 10,000 cubic metres of water above the natural level of any part of the surrounding land (in which case section 13 applies instead). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister may by order substitute a different volume of water for the volume specified in subsection (6A). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Certificates of construction engineers. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709103][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] As soon as the construction engineer responsible for any reservoir or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration *(Extent: E)*[^c-79b5261c45f17b237eabefa67d154018] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to a reservoir considers that the construction of the reservoir or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] has reached a stage at which the reservoir *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-66944f4361fe67ee9af066acf44fb710]... can properly be filled wholly or partially with water, he shall give a certificate (“the preliminary certificate”) specifying the level up to which it may be filled and the conditions (if any) subject to which it may be so filled; and the construction engineer may from time to time supersede a preliminary certificate by the issue of a further preliminary certificate varying the previous certificate, whether as to water level or as to conditions. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the construction engineer responsible for an *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration *(Extent: E)*[^c-47a992f8b4a3d27917b32525b88e7399] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to a large raised reservoir considers at any time during the carrying out of the alteration to the reservoir that the reservoir ought not to be filled with water up to the level or subject to the conditions that would be lawful apart from this subsection, he may give a certificate (“an interim certificate”) specifying the level up to which it may be filled until the issue of a preliminary certificate, and the conditions (if any) subject to which it may be so filled; and the construction engineer may from time to time supersede an interim certificate by the issue of a further interim certificate varying the previous certificate, whether as to water level or as to conditions. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If, at the end of three years after a preliminary certificate is first issued for a reservoir or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration *(Extent: E)*[^c-2a3804f7881fba1f3dea98f2a2218076] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to a reservoir or at any time thereafter, the construction engineer is satisfied that the reservoir *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-5c7a4e4f45840dd185583551ba8b92d8]... is sound and satisfactory and may safely be used for the storage of water, he shall give a certificate (“the final certificate”) to that effect, and the final certificate shall specify the level up to which water may be stored and the conditions (if any) subject to which it may be so stored. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If at the end of five years after a preliminary certificate is first issued for a reservoir or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration *(Extent: E)*[^c-414275dd4802e77b75384ec66b5e91db] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to a reservoir the construction engineer has not issued his final certificate, he shall give the undertakers a written explanation of his reasons for deferring the issue of the final certificate. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The construction engineer giving a final certificate for a reservoir *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] which is designated, or in the opinion of the engineer is likely to be designated, as a high-risk reservoir *(Extent: E)*[^c-b58ddcd36a1753c02199b46cdbbd9f56] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] shall consider the matters (if any) that need to be watched by a supervising engineer during the period before there is an inspection of the reservoir under this Act, and shall include in an annex to the certificate a note of any such matters. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The construction engineer for any reservoir or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration *(Extent: E)*[^c-8fb1bb485c2991baa848b8341d6fbc4f] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to a reservoir shall also, as soon as practicable after the completion of the works and in any event not later than the giving of the final certificate, give a certificate that the works have been efficiently executed in accordance with the drawings and descriptions annexed to the certificate, and shall annex to the certificate detailed drawings and descriptions giving full information of the works actually constructed, including dimensions and levels and details of the geological strata or deposits encountered in trail holes or excavations made in connection with the works. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] References in this Act to a final certificate or to a certificate under subsection (6) above include the annex to the certificate, where there is one. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Powers of enforcement authority in event of non-compliance with requirements as to construction or enlargement of reservoirs. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709104][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to the enforcement authority either— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that a large raised reservoir is being constructed (whether as a new reservoir or by the alteration of an *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] existing structure or area *(Extent: E)*[^c-2ce90c1b8c1730eb8b7e2ad122149e6b] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that is not a large raised reservoir) or is being altered so as to increase *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or decrease *(Extent: E)*[^c-5cecfcfbfb5d03d72641e7ac94058340] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] its capacity; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that, a large raised reservoir having been so constructed or altered, no final certificate has yet been given for the reservoir on the construction or alteration, as the case may be; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

but that no qualified civil engineer is responsible for the reservoir or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration *(Extent: E)*[^c-19f8e423821cfbcc787804f1f70703b5] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] as construction engineer, the authority may by written notice served on the undertakers require them within twenty eight days after the date when the notice is served to appoint a qualified civil engineer for the purposes of this section, unless an appointment has already been made, and (in either case) to notify the authority of the appointment. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An engineer appointed for the purposes of this section shall be appointed to inspect the reservoir and make a report on the construction or alteration, and to supervise the reservoir until he gives a final certificate for the reservoir under this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An engineer acting under this section shall include in his report any recommendations he sees fit to make as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety; and, subject to any reference of the matter to a referee in accordance with this Act, the undertakers shall carry any such recommendation into effect *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] within the period which the report must specify in respect of each recommendation. *(Extent: E)*[^c-4ce675dc69b63517bd7248b2ae096ff7] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3A (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-aca093381538e11e66977d66f829a2b3][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to the enforcement authority that the report of an engineer acting under this section includes a recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety that has not been carried into effect as required by this section, the authority may by written notice served on the undertakers require them to carry the recommendation into effect within a time specified in the notice. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3B (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where an enforcement authority propose to serve such a notice, the authority shall consult as to the time to be specified in the notice a civil engineer, being a qualified civil engineer for the purpose of inspecting and supervising the reservoir under this section. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Except as provided by subsections (5) and (6) below, an engineer acting under this section shall have the same powers and duties in relation to the giving of preliminary certificates, interim certificates and final certificates as if he were the construction engineer responsible for the reservoir or, as the case may be, the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration[^c-80a3c3b68716194148ef01c424dfc14c] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to it; and certificates under this subsection shall have effect for purposes of this Act as if they were certificates of a construction engineer. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A final certificate under this section may be given less than three years after the first issue of a preliminary certificate, or without the previous issue of a preliminary certificate, if the engineer is satisfied that the reservoir *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-a906cbeed8264d6ca1c9c36e60f1e647]... has for a period of three years or more been filled with water up to the level that is specified in the preliminary certificate or, if no preliminary certificate has been issued, up to the level that is specified in the final certificate, and that the reservoir *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-e540ed37651b523dc416a93683b794e3]... is sound and satisfactory and may safely be used for the storage of water. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A final certificate under this section, other than one given by virtue of subsection (5) above, shall not be required to state that the engineer is satisfied the reservoir *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-608eac5b48f9f9929bc142fe83b6af85]... is sound and satisfactory; but, if it does not do so and the engineer’s report includes any recommendations as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety, the certificate shall instead state that those recommendations have been carried into effect. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In addition to certificates under subsection (4) above, an engineer acting under this section, shall, as soon as practicable after the completion of the works and in any event not later than the giving of the final certificate, give a certificate that, so far as he has been able to ascertain, the works have been efficiently executed in accordance with the drawings and descriptions annexed to the certificate, and shall annex to the certificate detailed drawings and descriptions giving such information as he can of the works actually constructed, including dimensions and levels and details of the geological strata or deposits encountered in trial holes or excavations made in connection with the works *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] References in this Act to a final certificate under this section or to a certificate under subsection (7) above include the annex to the certificate, where there is one. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Re-use of abandoned reservoirs. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709105]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the use of a large raised reservoir as a reservoir has been abandoned (whether before or after the commencement of this Act), the reservoir shall not again be used as a reservoir unless a qualified civil engineer has been employed to inspect the reservoir and make a report on it, and to supervise the reservoir until he gives a final certificate for the reservoir under this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where a large raised reservoir is brought back into use as a reservoir after that use had been abandoned, it shall not be used for the storage of water, or be filled wholly or partially with water, otherwise than in accordance with the certificate of the engineer acting under this section (unless, on a subsequent alteration to it, section 6(4) above applies). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An engineer acting under this section shall include in his report any recommendations he see fit to make as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety; and, subject to any reference of the matter to a referee in accordance with this Act, the reservoir shall not be used as such if any such recommendation has not been carried into effect. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Except as provided by subsection (5) below, an engineer acting under this section shall have the same powers and duties in relation to the giving of preliminary certificates and final certificates as if he were the construction engineer responsible on the construction of the reservoir; and certificates under this subsection shall have effect for purposes of this Act as if they were certificates of a construction engineer. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A final certificate under this section shall not be required to state that the engineer is satisfied that the reservoir is sound and satisfactory; but, if it does not do so and the engineer’s report includes any recommendations as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety, the certificate shall instead state that those recommendations have been carried into effect. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Nothing in this section shall apply in relation to a reservoir, if before it is brought back into use, either— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] it is altered in such manner as is to be treated for purposes of this Act as the construction of a new reservoir; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] it is altered under the supervision of a qualified civil engineer so as not to be a large raised reservoir when brought back into use. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to the enforcement authority— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that a large raised reservoir has been brought back into use as a reservoir after that used had been abandoned but that a report has not been obtained as required by this section; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that a report obtained under this section on a reservoir includes a recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety that has not been carried into effect as required by this section; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

the authority may by written notice served on the undertakers require them within twenty-eight days after the date when the notice is served to appoint a qualified civil engineer for the purposes of this section, unless an appointment has already been made, and (in either case) to notify the authority of the appointment or, as the case may be, require them to carry the recommendation into effect within a time specified in the notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where an enforcement authority propose to serve a notice under subsection (7) above requiring undertakers to carry a recommendation into effect, the authority shall consult as to the time to be specified in the notice a civil engineer, being a qualified civil engineer for the purpose of inspecting and supervising the reservoir under this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## Inspections (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 10 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Periodical inspection of large raised reservoirs. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d8bd7b1d46da513c0a28dd8f1892364a][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709106][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertakers shall have any *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] high-risk reservoir[^c-14c4f5978761a060bda367d8f6f9c8d6] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] inspected from time to time by an independent qualified civil engineer (“the inspecting engineer”) and obtain from him a report of the result of his inspection. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Unless it is at the time under the supervision of a construction engineer (or of an engineer acting under section 8 or 9 above) a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] high-risk reservoir must be inspected under this section at the times specified by regulations made by the Minister.[^c-9aac7b956368a85c5accb808fa1e1916] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] As soon as practicable after an inspection under this section, the inspecting engineer shall make a report of the result of the inspection, including in it any recommendations he sees fit to make as to— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ef0594a003ac46bffef9c6d65463a46b][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the time of the next inspection; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the maintenance of the reservoir; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any measures required in the interests of safety and the period within which those measures must be taken. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-fb4abe10f4cc6379a9b619b46d483eca][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If the inspecting engineer has not provided a report before the end of the period of 6 months beginning with the date of completion of the inspection, the engineer must— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] notify *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the appropriate agency[^c-4187307572d4df8eadc0f4f66d346fd3] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] provide a written statement of the reasons. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An inspecting engineer shall consider the matters (if any) that need to be watched by the supervising engineer during the period before the next inspection of the reservoir under this section, and shall include in his report a note of any such matters. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An inspecting engineer, when he makes his report, shall also give a certificate stating that the report does or does not include recommendations as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or as to the maintenance of the reservoir[^c-b08acdda9d7a8598402f5267486b9535] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], if it includes a recommendation as to the time of the next inspection, stating also the period within which he recommends the inspection should be made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-733f19d946d5a79320475f34fe04170b][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertaker must comply with a recommendation made under subsection (3)(b), unless the recommendation is the subject of a reference under section 19 and the reference has not been determined. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where an inspecting engineer includes in his report any recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety, then subject to any reference of the matter to a referee in accordance with this Act the undertakers shall *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , within the period specified in the report,[^c-0889f084c753fd35f636387dae969cee] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] carry the recommendation into effect under the supervision of a qualified civil engineer; and that engineer shall give a certificate, as soon as he is satisfied it is so, that the recommendation has been carried into effect. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc613bf629680f1d30ca9ac8342a425a][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The inspecting engineer must include in the report of the inspection— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] a statement as to whether all of the safety measures recommended in the previous report under subsection (3)(c) have been taken, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] either (i) recommendations to take any safety measure that has not yet been taken or (ii) an explanation of why it is no longer required. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to the enforcement authority, in the case of any *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] high-risk reservoir[^c-72f1572a1fb4fa0464884e50498a5a3c] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that an inspection and report thereon have not been made as required by this section; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that the latest report of the inspecting engineer includes a recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety that has not been carried into effect as so required; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

the authority may by written notice served on the undertakers require them within twenty-eight days after the date when the notice is served to appoint an independent qualified civil engineer to carry out an inspection under this section, unless an appointment has already been made, and (in either case) to notify the authority of the appointment or, as the case may be, require them to carry the recommendation into effect within a time specified in the notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where an enforcement authority propose to serve a notice under subsection (7) above requiring the undertakers to carry a recommendation into effect, the authority shall consult as to the time to be specified in the notice a civil engineer, being a qualified civil engineer for the purpose of supervising under subsection (6) above the carrying into effect of the recommendation. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] For purposes of this Act “independent” when used of a civil engineer in relation to a reservoir means— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that he is not in the employment of the undertakers otherwise than in a consultant capacity; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that he was not the engineer responsible for the reservoir or any *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] alteration *(Extent: E)*[^c-41198c89204513f7199c79f6f5ad12bb] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to it as construction engineer, nor is connected with any such engineer as his partner, employer, employee or fellow employee in a civil engineering business. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

The reference in this subsection to a construction engineer includes an engineer acting under section 8 or 9 above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## Monitoring and supervision (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 11 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Recording of water levels (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) etc. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709107][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] For every *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] high-risk reservoir *(Extent: E)*[^c-b9543babf276cbe9dfcad55e4f343885] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the undertakers shall keep a record in the prescribed form of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] water levels and depth of water, including the flow of water over the waste weir or overflow; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] leakages, settlements of walls or other works, and repairs; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] such other matters as may be prescribed; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

and shall install and maintain such instruments as may be needed to provide the information to be recorded. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The record to be kept for a reservoir under this section shall give such information as may be prescribed of any of the matters to be included in the record, and shall give it at such intervals and in such manner as may from time to time be required by any directions of the construction engineer or inspecting engineer. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 12 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Supervision of large raised reservoirs. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709108][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] At all times when a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] high-risk reservoir *(Extent: E)*[^c-5d7090d174ea670030ee5cee482a6646] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] is not under the supervision of a construction engineer, a qualified civil engineer (“ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**the supervising engineer *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**”) shall be employed to supervise the reservoir and keep the undertakers advised of its behaviour in any respect that might affect safety, and to watch that the provisions of section 6(2) to (4) or section 9(2) above and of section 11 are observed and complied with and draw the attention of the undertakers to any breach of those provisions. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] It shall be the duty of the supervising engineer, so long as any matters are noted as matters that need to be watched by him in any annex to the final certificate for the reservoir or in the latest report of an inspecting engineer, to pay attention in particular to those matters and to give the undertakers not less often than once a year written statement of the action he has taken to do so. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2A (Extent: E) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-904dd5ddcfdd6324d4544ea2ebf71e14][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The supervising engineer must provide the undertaker with a written statement of any steps taken to maintain the reservoir in accordance with the recommendations of the inspecting engineer under section 10(3)(b). *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2B (Extent: E) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The engineer must provide a statement under subsection (2A) at least once every 12 months. *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The supervising engineer shall recommend to the undertakers that the reservoir be inspected under section 10 above, if at any time he thinks that such an inspection is called for. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to the enforcement authority that a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] high-risk reservoir *(Extent: E)*[^c-b06a0b4186c1273780258c9b5951a9c6] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] is not for the time being under the supervision either of a construction engineer or of a supervising engineer, the authority may by written notice served on the undertakers require them within twenty-eight days after the date the notice is served to appoint a supervising engineer and to notify the authority of the appointment or, if the reservoir is at that date under the supervision of a construction engineer or of a supervising engineer, to notify the authority of that fact. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] References in this section to a construction engineer include an engineer acting under section 8 or 9 above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: E) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c7d4bdbc6fada285d3682040b2649e6f][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The supervising engineer may direct the undertaker to carry out a visual inspection of the reservoir at specified intervals for the purpose of identifying anything that might affect the safety of the reservoir. *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: E) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertaker must notify the supervising engineer of— *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: E) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] each visual inspection that is carried out, and *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: E) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] anything noticed in the course of it. *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: E) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister may issue guidance about supervision in accordance with this section (and may take compliance into account when making decisions under section 4). *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## Flooding (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-9964ebf7fce1c62e3ba99f4c13c9b669][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
