(1 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Secretary of State may, by written notice served on the undertakers in relation to a large raised reservoir, direct them to prepare a plan (a “flood plan”) *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-16224203f2789d2bcf6c3a30ebab83be]... *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E*

(1A (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d572fe4c91ebf668cabdfc4ac8208b98][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A flood plan is a document— *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] giving information about the areas that may be flooded in the event of an uncontrolled escape of water from the large raised reservoir, *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] specifying the action that the undertaker would take in order to prevent an uncontrolled escape of water, and *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] specifying the action that the undertaker would take in order to control or mitigate the effects of a flood. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A direction may in particular— *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] specify the matters to be included in the flood plan; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] require the flood plan to be prepared in accordance with such methods of technical or other analysis as may be specified by the Environment Agency *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or the[^c-d4613df0e59776634f0dd58d421d3873] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] require the flood plan, or any information about the matters contained in it, to be given to the Environment Agency *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or the[^c-fa0737cbd228c3aee8132f69113f147e] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] at such time or times as may be directed by that Agency *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , by the[^c-955268b291a5efaf295ff5906b795575] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or by the Secretary of State; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (d (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] require a copy of the flood plan to be sent to such persons as may be specified in the direction; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (e (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] require publication of the flood plan, in such manner as may be specified in the direction, for the purpose of bringing the matters contained in the flood plan to the attention of persons likely to be interested. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Before giving a direction under this section the Secretary of State shall consult— *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the undertakers concerned; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the Environment Agency *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] if the reservoir concerned is in England or any of the flooding to which the plan relates would be in England[^c-5dccaa040a655a475fd1828f6843a95e] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (ba (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-93829de90df4db541ae1551cc4d802f2][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] NRBW *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] if the reservoir concerned is in Wales or any of the flooding to which the plan relates would be in Wales; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] if the reservoir concerned is in England, the county council, metropolitan district council or London borough council in whose area the reservoir is situated; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (d (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] if the reservoir concerned is in Wales, the county council or county borough council in whose area the reservoir is situated; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (e (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] such persons appearing to the Secretary of State to represent the emergency services in the area where the reservoir is situated; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (f (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] such other persons (if any) as the Secretary of State considers appropriate. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

he may give a direction under that subsection in relation to that reservoir. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 12AA (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Flood plans: preparation (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-6a9401b99aa6d84fbbcf479967a37a92][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] This section applies where an undertaker is directed to prepare a flood plan under section 12A. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertaker must prepare a flood plan in consultation with the appointed engineer. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertaker may not provide a copy of, or publish, a flood plan in accordance with a direction under section 12A(2)(d) or (e) unless the appointed engineer has certified that the requirements of a direction under section 12A(2)(a) and (b) are satisfied. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertaker must test a flood plan at such times and in such manner as may be directed by the appointed engineer. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In the event of flooding, or if flooding is reasonably expected to occur, the undertaker must implement the flood plan without delay. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertaker— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] must keep a flood plan under review, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] may revise a flood plan. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertaker must revise the flood plan in accordance with the directions of the appointed engineer. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The following apply to a revision of a flood plan as they apply to a flood plan— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any requirements of a direction under section 12A. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In this section “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**appointed engineer *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**” means— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] in the case of a high-risk reservoir, the supervising engineer, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] in any other case, the engineer appointed for the purposes of this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 12B (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Flood plans and national security (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d3a0249387bbdc285f0da9d7c12c2016][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If it appears to the Secretary of State that in the interests of national security any person or class of persons referred to in any one or more of paragraphs (a) to (e) of section 12A(3) above should not be consulted about a proposed direction, he may treat that subsection as not referring to that person or to that class of person. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In relation to any reservoir (whether *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] large or raised *(Extent: E)*[^c-bc501a0220360645f34b1624f666035c] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or not, as the case may be) the Secretary of State may, by written notice served on the undertakers, require them not to publish, or not to publish except as specified in the notice— *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] a flood plan prepared by them pursuant to a notice given under section 12A above; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any corresponding plan prepared by them other than pursuant to such a notice, *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

and a notice under this subsection may also require the undertakers to withhold access to any such plan from any person except as specified in the notice. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## Discontinuance or abandonment (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 13 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Discontinuance of large raised reservoirs. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709109][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-f1d3933a34e61bef6fdcaf0ed4da0c29][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] No large raised reservoir shall be altered in order to render it incapable of holding *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] 10,000 cubic metres of water above the natural level of any part of the surrounding land *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-bc3c6f8ea9d47aa3808952710ebaf3e3] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], unless a qualified civil engineer is employed to design or approve and to supervise the alteration. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

(1A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bb20745e84032d2600a6e9896e47d75a][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An engineer employed for the purposes of subsection (1) may issue a certificate (the “interim certificate”) if the engineer thinks that the level of water in the reservoir should be reduced before the alteration is completed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1B (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The interim certificate must specify— *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the reduced water level, *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the time by which it must be reduced, and *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the conditions (if any) on which the reservoir may be filled to the reduced level. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1C (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The undertaker must ensure that the reservoir does not contain water except in accordance with the interim certificate. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1D (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The engineer employed for the purposes of subsection (1) may vary an interim certificate by giving written notice to the undertaker. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1E (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An interim certificate ceases to have effect on the issue of a certificate under subsection (2). *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An engineer employed for the purposes of subsection (1) above shall give a certificate, as soon as he is satisfied it is so, that the alteration has been completed and has been efficiently executed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-f1d3933a34e61bef6fdcaf0ed4da0c29][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where a certificate is given under subsection (2) above, a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority[^c-3f14a13792f6781f6a826fee8d9a611b] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] on receipt of the certificate or a copy of it shall remove the reservoir from their register of large raised reservoirs; but a reservoir that has been a large raised reservoir but is altered so as no longer to be capable of holding *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] 10,000 cubic metres of water above the natural level of any part of the surrounding land *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-42d76f562b754896000515ecf2c05101] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] shall nevertheless continue for purposes of this Act to be a large raised reservoir, unless the alteration is made and a certificate given in accordance with this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-246e874b5aa906e42267865239ce2874][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister may by order substitute a different volume of water for the volume specified in subsection (1) or (3). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the appropriate agency[^c-248447f53e323b14fc4e57d156f29a83] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that a qualified civil engineer has not been employed as required by subsection (1) *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the appropriate agency *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] may by notice require the undertaker— *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to appoint a qualified civil engineer for the purposes of this section before the end of the period of 28 days beginning with the day on which the notice is given, unless the appointment has already been made, and *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] to notify *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] the appropriate agency *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] of the appointment (whether it was made before or after the notice was given). *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 14 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Abandonment of large raised reservoirs. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709110][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the use of a large raised reservoir as a reservoir is to be abandoned, the undertakers shall obtain from a qualified civil engineer a report as to the measures (if any) that ought to be taken in the interests of safety to secure that the reservoir is incapable of filling accidentally or naturally with water above the natural level of any part of the land adjoining the reservoir or is only capable of doing so to an extent that does not constitute a risk. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the report of an engineer under this section makes any recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety, then subject to any reference of the matter to a referee in accordance with this Act the undertakers obtaining the report shall, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] within the period specified in the report *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-2f434c1546057e5dc90e97ae5888d27e] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], carry the recommendation into effect; and if the recommendation involves any alteration of the reservoir, section 13 above shall apply accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The engineer from whom a report is obtained under this section shall give with it a certificate stating that the report does or does not make recommendations for measures to be taken in the interests of safety. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to the enforcement authority, in the case of any large raised reservoir,— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that the use of the reservoir as a reservoir has been abandoned but that a report has not been obtained as required by this section; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that a report obtained under this section includes a recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety that has not been carried into effect as required by this section; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

the authority may by written notice served on the undertakers require them within twenty-eight days after the date when the notice is served to appoint a qualified civil engineer to make the report under this section, unless an appointment has already been made, and (in either case) to notify the authority of the appointment or, as the case may be, require them to carry the recommendation into effect within a time specified in the notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

References in this subsection, and in any other provision of this Act as it operates in relation thereto, to the enforcement authority or to the undertakers shall have effect as at the time when the use of the reservoir as such is abandoned. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where an enforcement authority propose to serve a notice under subsection (4) above requiring undertakers to carry a recommendation into effect, the authority shall consult as to the time to be specified in the notice a civil engineer, who, if the recommendation involves any alteration of the reservoir, shall be a qualified civil engineer for the purpose of supervising the alteration under section 13 above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-fd9ecb334a293cf13597bda9b8d82042][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Minister may by regulations make provision about what is and is not to be treated for the purposes of this Act as— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] abandonment of use of a large raised reservoir as a reservoir, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] bringing a large raised reservoir back into use as a reservoir. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## Additional powers of enforcement authority (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 15 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Reserve powers. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709111][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where undertakers are required by a notice from the enforcement authority under section 8, 9, 10, 12 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , 13 *(Extent: E)*[^c-9dd48a22eba8cd66064190cd208bb2de] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or 14 above to appoint an engineer for any purpose of this Act, and the undertakers fail to make the appointment, the authority may appoint an engineer for that purpose, being a person eligible for appointment by the undertakers; and, except as otherwise provided, the provisions of this Act shall apply in relation to any person appointed under this section and to anything done by him as if he had been duly appointed by the undertakers. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where undertakers are required by a notice from the enforcement authority under section *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] 8, *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-27121b7cb9ea2d5674f4a1821b90dcd6] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] 9, 10 or 14 above to carry into effect any recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety, and the undertakers fail to comply with that requirement, the authority may cause the recommendation to be carried into effect under the supervision of a qualified civil engineer appointed by them, who shall give a certificate, as soon as he is satisfied that it is so, that the recommendation has been carried into effect. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An appointment made under subsection (1) above for any purpose of this Act shall be of no effect, if before it is made the undertakers have appointed for that purpose an engineer eligible to be so appointed; and an appointment under that subsection of a supervising engineer shall terminate when an appointment of a supervising engineer duly made by the undertakers takes effect. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Without prejudice to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] section 123 of the Environment Act 1995 as it is applied by section 22A of this Act[^c-326deebb98557c6c518c8bb7ec1beb28] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or section 192 of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709113]Local Government (Scotland) Act 1973, where the enforcement authority are unable after reasonable enquiry to ascertain the name or address of the undertakers for any large raised reservoir, then for purposes of this section a notice relating to the reservoir shall be deemed to have been duly served on the undertakers if it has been left in the hands of a person who is or appears to be resident or employed at the reservoir or if it has been left conspicuously affixed to some building or object at the reservoir. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where an enforcement authority make any appointment under subsection (1) above or exercise powers conferred by subsection (2), the undertakers shall pay them the amount of the expenses reasonably incurred by them by reason of the appointment or, as the case may be, in the exercise of those powers. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 16 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Emergency powers. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709114]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to the enforcement authority, in the case of any large raised reservoir, that the reservoir is unsafe and that immediate action is needed to protect persons or property against an escape of water from the reservoir, they may take at the reservoir such measures as they consider proper to remove or reduce the risk or to mitigate the effects of an escape. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where it appears to the enforcement authority, in the case of any large raised reservoir, that the use of the reservoir as a reservoir has been abandoned, but that there may from time to time be an undue accumulation of water there and immediate action is needed to protect persons or property against an escape of water, they may take there such measures as they consider proper to remove or reduce the risk or to mitigate the effects of an escape. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An enforcement authority proposing to exercise the powers conferred by this section shall appoint a qualified civil engineer to make recommendations as to the measures to be taken in the exercise of those powers; and any measures so taken shall be carried into effect under the supervision of a qualified civil engineer appointed by the authority. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Subject to subsection (5) below, an enforcement authority exercising or proposing to exercise at a reservoir the powers conferred by this section shall, as early as practicable, serve on the undertakers a notice giving full information of the measures that are being or are to be taken in the exercise of those powers; and if that notice cannot be given before the work is begun, the authority shall notify the undertakers as early as practicable of the beginning of the work. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Subsection (4) above shall not require an enforcement authority to serve any notice on the undertakers after work is begun at the reservoir, if the authority are unable after reasonable enquiry to ascertain the name or address of the undertakers; and in relation to notices served before work is begun section 15(4) above shall apply for purposes of this section as it applies for the purposes of that. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where an enforcement authority exercise the powers conferred by this section, the undertakers shall pay them the amount of the expenses reasonably incurred by them in the exercise of those powers. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] For purposes of subsection (2) above references in this section, and in any other provision of this Act as it operates in relation thereto, to the enforcement authority or to the undertakers shall have effect as at the time when the use of the reservoir as such is abandoned. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 17 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Powers of entry. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709115][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Subject to the following provisions of this section a person duly authorised in writing by an enforcement authority may at any reasonable time enter upon the land on which a reservoir is situated— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] for the purpose of carrying out an inspection, survey or other operation to determine whether any provision of this Act applies; *(Extent: E)*[^c-391972f13863dd55ecf5fa5650bdfbcf] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] for the purpose of carrying out any survey or other operation needed to determine whether any recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , or as to the maintenance of the reservoir, *(Extent: E)*[^c-77d83d76d438867352c390ee954fd65b] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] has been carried into effect as required by section *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] 8, *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-3b6d1fc92222c72e470af1777b80e3ed] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] 9, 10 or 14 above or what period should be specified in a notice under section *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] 8, *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] 9, 10 or 14 requiring the undertakers to carry such a recommendation into effect; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] for the purpose of carrying out any inspection of the reservoir that he has been appointed under section 15(1) above to carry out, or any survey or other operation needed for the purpose of a report that he has been appointed under section 15(1) to make; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] for any purpose connected with the carrying into effect under section 15(2) above of a recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] for the purpose of carrying out any survey or other operation needed to determine whether any or what measures should be taken under section 16 above, or for any purpose connected with the carrying into effect of any measures taken under that section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the entry is for a purpose within subsection (1)(e) above, the power to enter upon the land on which a reservoir is situated shall extend also to any neighbouring land. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the use of a large raised reservoir as a reservoir is abandoned, subsection (1) above (so far as material) shall continue to apply in relation to the site of the reservoir as land on which a reservoir is situated; and for this purpose references in subsection (1), and in any other provision of this Act as it operates in relation thereto, to the enforcement authority or to the undertakers shall have effect as at the time when the use of the reservoir as such is abandoned. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Except for a purpose within subsection (1)(e) above, a person may not under this section demand admission as of right to any land that is occupied, unless at least seven days’ notice in writing of the intended entry has been given to the occupier or the entry is authorised by a warrant granted under subsection (5) below. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

A notice under this subsection shall specify the purpose for which entry is required and shall indicate so far as practicable the nature of any works to be executed on the land. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If it is shown to the satisfaction of a justice of the peace on sworn information in writing— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that admission to any land on which any person is entitled to enter under this section has been refused to that person, or that a refusal is apprehended, or that the occupier is temporarily absent; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] that there is reasonable ground for entry on to the land for the purpose for which entry is required; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

the justice may by warrant under his hand authorise that person to enter on the land, if need be by force; but such a warrant shall not be issued on the ground that entry has been refused or that a refusal of entry is apprehended unless the justice is satisfied that notice in writing of the intention to apply for a warrant has been given to the occupier. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Every warrant granted under this section shall continue in force until the purpose for which the entry is required has been satisfied. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A person duly authorised to enter on any land by virtue of this section shall, if so required, produce evidence of his authority before so entering and may take with him on to the land such other persons and such equipment as may be necessary. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any person who wilfully obstructs a person entitled to enter on land by virtue of this section shall be liable on summary conviction to a fine not exceeding *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] level 3 on the standard scale[^c709116] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In the application of this section to Scotland— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any reference to a justice of the peace includes a reference to the sheriff having jurisdiction in the place where the land is situated; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] for the reference to be sworn information in writing there shall be substituted a reference to evidence on oath. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 18 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Compensation to third parties for damage or disturbance. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709117]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where in the exercise in relation to any reservoir of powers conferred by section 17 above— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any land on which entry is made and which is not in the occupation of the undertakers is damaged; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any person is disturbed in his enjoyment of any such land; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

any person interested in the land which is damaged or, as the case may be, the person whose enjoyment of land is disturbed shall be entitled to receive compensation from the enforcement authority in respect of the damage or disturbance. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any dispute as to a right to compensation under this section or as to the amount of any such compensation shall be determined by the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Upper Tribunal or[^c-856cbc715e8a0506ee10d92c72492d32] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , if the land in question is in Scotland, by the Lands Tribunal for Scotland. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Compensation payable under this section by an enforcement authority shall for the purpose of recovery from the undertakers be treated as an expense incurred in the exercise of the powers conferred by section 16 above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## Supplementary (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] an inspecting engineer includes in his report recommendations as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , or as to the maintenance of the reservoir *(Extent: E)*[^c-13c092f7c8b2392f1546a43d3613ec64] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or as to the time of the next inspection; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] an engineer acting under section 8, 9 or 14 above includes in his report recommendations as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

the undertakers if aggrieved by any recommendation may, in accordance with rules under this section, refer their complaint to a referee. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

(1A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-305350fcd6a3fb6265e65bf0cc129a3b][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If an engineer determines that the requirements of a direction under section 12A(2)(a) and (b) are not satisfied for the purposes of section 12AA(3), the undertaker may refer the matter to a referee in accordance with rules under this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A referee under this section shall be an independent qualified civil engineer appointed by agreement between the undertakers and the engineer making the recommendation *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] or determination *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-daa25dd34d473bd2ac2edbdb663aa0d1] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] complained of, or in default of their agreement shall be a person appointed by the Secretary of State. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A referee under *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] subsection (1) *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-3bea2798dd98818839181cc3f388a304] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], after investigating the complaint, shall have power to make such modifications as he thinks fit in the report containing the recommendation complained of, and the report shall for the purposes of this Act have effect accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A referee under *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] subsection (1) *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-989cbe691161a0d1c2ae15ff6ee59a83] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], when he gives his decision on a report, shall also give a certificate stating that the decision does or does not modify the report, and (if necessary in consequence of any modification) revising accordingly any certificate given with reference to the report by the engineer making the report. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

(4A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-7b42abea97913a9d515082d775190825][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] A referee under subsection (1A) may direct the engineer to issue a certificate for the purposes of section 12AA(3). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The Secretary of State may by statutory instrument make rules as to the time within which a referee may be appointed by agreement under this section, as to the time within which, and the manner in which, a request for the appointment of a referee under this section may be made to him, as to the procedure before the referee and as to the costs of the proceedings before and investigation by the referee (including the remuneration of the referee), so, however, that those costs (including the remuneration of the referee) shall be paid by the undertakers. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] a requirement to carry a recommendation of an engineer into effect under sections 8(3A), 9(7), 10(7) and 14(4). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The regulations must— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] confer jurisdiction on the Minister, a court or a tribunal, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

General provisions as to reports, certificates (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) etc. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) of engineers. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709119][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any report *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , certificate or directions *(Extent: E+W)*[^c-b5eb7787eda21f023a249187657c1ad7] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] of an engineer acting for any purpose of this Act, including a referee under section 19 above, shall be in the prescribed form. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any such report or certificate, unless the engineer in question is appointed to act by the enforcement authority, shall be delivered to and kept by the undertakers. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Any such report or certificate, where the engineer in question is appointed to act by the enforcement authority, shall be delivered to the authority, but a copy of it shall be sent by the engineer at the same time to the undertakers and shall be kept by them. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where any document to which this subsection applies is delivered by the engineer in question to the undertakers, the engineer shall within twenty-eight days after he delivers it to the undertakers send a copy of it to the enforcement authority. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

The documents to which this subsection applies are— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any certificate of an engineer acting for any purpose of this Act; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any report made by an inspecting engineer or an engineer acting for purposes of section 14 above *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-cec8fff522036665056965d1b083de2d]... , and any report made by an engineer acting under section 8 or 9 above; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any decision of a referee *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c-317e66d3a3302e8296137576e71179ab]... ; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any written explanation given by a construction engineer to the undertakers of his reasons for deferring the issue of his final certificate; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] any advice given by a supervising engineer to the undertakers which either— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] recommends them to have the reservoir inspected under section 10 above or to take any other action; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where a large raised reservoir has been wholly or partly constructed before the commencement of this Act any certificate given, report made, or other thing done in relation to the reservoir under or for purposes of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709127]Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930 shall for the purposes of this Act have the same effect as if given, made or done under or for purposes of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The repeal by this Act of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709128]Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930 shall not affect the continuance in force of regulations or rules made under that Act or of any panel of qualified civil engineers constituted under that Act, but they shall continue as if made or consituted for corresponding purposes under this Act; but any appointment made to any such panel before the commencement of this Act shall lapse at the end of five years after the date of that commencement or, in the case of any engineer or engineers, at such earlier date as may be notified to him or them by the Secretary of State by written notice given not less than six months before that date. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

sections 6 to 8 above shall from that commencement apply as they apply in the case of a construction or alteration carried out wholly after that commencement. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Subsection (1)(b) above shall not apply where a final certificate was not given as there mentioned because the construction or alteration of the reservoir was completed before the commencement of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709134]Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930; and nothing in that subsection shall apply where before the commencement of this Act a qualified civil engineer (within the meaning of that Act) was not employed to design and supervise the construction or alteration of the reservoir because the reservoir was not one to which that Act applied. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If in a case within subsection (1) above there is at the commencement of this Act no qualified civil engineer responsible for the reservoir or addition as construction engineer, the undertakers shall appoint a qualified civil engineer for purposes of section 8 above without being required by a notice from the enforcement authority; and if they fail to do so within six months after the date of the commencement of this Act, sections 15 and 22(1) above shall apply as if the undertakers had been served with a notice under section 8 so as to require them to make the appointment by the end of those six months. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

If a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority[^c-96eea6ff829b3f3b5426e4cab67530a1] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] are the undertakers and the reservoir will be wholly in the area of that authority, then notwithstanding anything in section 2(6) above this subsection shall have effect in relation to the reservoir as it would have effect in relation to it if the undertakers were not a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , except that the reference to section 15 shall not apply. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where at the commencement of this Act a final certificate has been given under the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709135]Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930 on the construction of a large raised reservoir or on the alteration of a reservoir so as to increase its capacity, but the reservoir has not been inspected under that Act since the date of the certificate, section 10(2) above shall apply as if that certificate had been given on the date of the commencement of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 26 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Reservoirs constructed before commencement of previous Act. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709136]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the construction of a large raised reservoir (whether as a new reservoir or by the alteration of an existing reservoir that was not a large raised reservoir) was completed before the commencement of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709137]Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930 and the reservoir was not inspected under that Act then unless section 25(1) or (5) above applies by reason of any alteration of the reservoir increasing its capacity, the first inspection of the reservoir under this Act shall be made as soon as practicable after the commencement of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where the construction of a large raised reservoir (whether as a new reservoir or by the alteration of an existing reservoir that was not a large raised reservoir) was completed before the commencement of the Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930, then on the first inspection of the reservoir under this Act the inspecting engineer shall annex to his report drawings and descriptions giving, so far as he can, the like information of the works actually contructed at any time before the commencement of that Act (and not removed on any subsequent alteration increasing the capacity of the reservoir) as would have been annexed to a certificate under section 7(6) above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where subsection (2) above applies on the inspection of a reservoir, any reference in section 20 or section 21(5) above to an inspecting engineer’s report shall include the annex, and section 20(4)(b) shall apply whether or not the report is stated in the inspecting engineer’s certificate to include a recommendation as to measures to be taken in the interests of safety. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 27 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Large raised reservoirs not within previous Act. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709138]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Where before the commencement of this Act a qualified civil engineer within the meaning of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709139]Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930 has not been employed to design and supervise the construction of a large raised reservoir or an alteration of a reservoir to increase its capacity because the reservoir was not one to which that Act applied, then— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] notwithstanding that the construction or alteration is not completed at the commencement of this Act, section 6(1) and (5) and section 7 above shall not apply except in so far as section 7 is applied by section 8; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] whether or not the construction or alteration is so completed, the undertakers shall appoint a qualified civil engineer for purposes of section 8 without being required by a notice from the enforcement authority; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] if the construction or alteration is so completed, section 6(2) to (4) shall apply in accordance with section 8 as they apply in the case of a construction or alteration carried out wholly after the commencement of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If the undertakers fail within six months after the date of the commencement of this Act to appoint a qualified civil engineer as required by subsection (1)(b) above, sections 15 and 22(1) above shall apply as if the undertakers had been served with a notice under section 8 so as to require them to make the appointment by the end of those six months. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority[^c-85714c4519e6bdddea720d0e90299e10] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] are the undertakers and the reservoir is situated wholly in the area of that authority, then notwithstanding anything in section 2(6) above this section shall have effect in relation to the reservoir as it would have effect in relation to it if the undertakers were not a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] relevant authority *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] , except that the reference to section 15 in subsection (2) above shall not apply. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## Crown application (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-a7e6984229fd919a486c804e3c181bf6][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 27A (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Crown application (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(1 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Subject to the provisions of this section, this Act binds the Crown. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] No contravention by the Crown of any provision made by or under this Act shall make the Crown criminally liable; but the High Court may, on the application of the *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] appropriate agency[^c-2295215904eab832838d437fcf489644] *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb], declare unlawful any act or omission of the Crown which constitutes such a contravention. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(3 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Notwithstanding anything in subsection (2) above, the provisions of this Act shall apply to persons in the public service of the Crown as they apply to other persons. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(4 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] If the Secretary of State certifies that it appears to him, as respects any Crown premises and any power of entry exercisable in relation to them specified in the certificate, that it is requisite or expedient that, in the interests of national security, the powers should not be exercisable in relation to those premises, those powers shall not be exercisable in relation to those premises. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(5 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Subject to subsection (4) above, the power conferred by section 17 above shall be exercisable in relation to land in which there is a Crown or Duchy interest only with the consent of the appropriate authority. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(6 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Nothing in this section shall be taken as in any way affecting Her Majesty in Her private capacity; and this subsection shall be construed as if section 38(3) of the Crown Proceedings Act 1947 (interpretation of references to Her Majesty in Her private capacity) were contained in this Act. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(7 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] In this section— *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

- “ *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**the appropriate authority *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**” has the same meaning as it has in Part 13 of the Town and Country Planning Act 1990 by virtue of section 293(2) of that Act; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- “ *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**Crown or Duchy interest *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**” means an interest which belongs to Her Majesty in right of the Crown or of the Duchy of Lancaster, or to the Duchy of Cornwall, or belonging to a government department or held in trust for Her Majesty for the purposes of a government department; *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
- “ *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**Crown premises *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]**” means premises held by or on behalf of the Crown. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(8 (Extent: E+W) (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] The provisions of subsection (3) of section 293 of the Town and Country Planning Act 1990 (questions relating to Crown application) as to the determination of questions shall apply for the purposes of this section. *(Extent: E+W)* *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 27C (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Ministerial responsibility (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-2e997cb80e808225e2ef830eabede389][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

*Extent: E+W*

References to “the Minister” in this Act are to be construed in accordance with paragraph 42 of Schedule 4 to the Flood and Water Management Act 2010. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## Repeal, commencement (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) etc. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 28 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Repeal. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709140]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] There are hereby repealed the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709141]Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930 and the entry relating to section 5(1) of that Act in Part II of Schedule 9 to the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709142]Courts Act 1971. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] Notwithstanding the repeal of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] [^c709143]Reservoirs (Safety Provisions) Act 1930, the provision made by section 7 of that Act shall continue to have effect as reproduced in Schedule 2 to this Act with certain changes in the wording of section 7 (to avoid mention in terms of the commencement or the passing of that Act or of the undertakers); but the inclusion of that provision in this Act shall not affect its operation, and in particular sections 1(2) and 6(1) above shall not apply for the interpretation of Schedule 2. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 29 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Commencement. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c709144]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c709145][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] This Act shall come into force on such a day as may be appointed by order of the Secretary of State made by statutory instrument, and the day appointed may differ for different provisions of the Act or for different purposes of the same provision. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb] An order of the Secretary of State under this section may adapt references in this Act to its commencement in such manner as appears to the Secretary of State to be required by the operation of the order in bringing any provision of the Act into force; but subject to any such adaptation a reference in any provision of this Act to its commencement shall be construed for any purpose of that provision as a reference to the coming into force of the provision for that purpose. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

### 30 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Short title and extent. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

## SCHEDULE 2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — Liability for Damage and Injury (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c709146][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb][^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

Section 28. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]

Where damage or injury is caused by the escape of water from a reservoir constructed after the year 1930 under statutory powers granted after July 1930, the fact that the reservoir was so constructed shall not exonerate the persons for the time being having the management and control of the reservoir from any indictment, action or other proceedings to which they would otherwise have been liable. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dc6fc133c055b00f4a5f9ad75dbeaceb]
